arazzos:
- description: Confirm a subscription is enabled, list all its resources, and read its predefined tag names.
  name: Microsoft Azure Audit Subscription Resources
  slug: microsoft-azure-audit-subscription-resources-workflow
- description: Create a resource group then deploy an ARM template referenced by URI, polling to completion.
  name: Microsoft Azure Create Resource Group and Deploy From Template Link
  slug: microsoft-azure-create-resource-group-and-deploy-from-link-workflow
- description: Create a resource group then read it back to confirm it provisioned successfully.
  name: Microsoft Azure Create Resource Group and Verify
  slug: microsoft-azure-create-resource-group-and-verify-workflow
- description: Create a resource group, start an ARM template deployment, and poll until it succeeds.
  name: Microsoft Azure Deploy Template to a New Resource Group
  slug: microsoft-azure-deploy-template-to-resource-group-workflow
- description: Create a group, deploy a template, poll to success, then delete the whole group.
  name: Microsoft Azure Deploy Then Tear Down a Resource Group
  slug: microsoft-azure-deploy-then-delete-resource-group-workflow
- description: Get a deployment, confirm it succeeded, then list the resources in its resource group.
  name: Microsoft Azure Deployment Status and Resulting Resources
  slug: microsoft-azure-deployment-status-and-resource-list-workflow
- description: Filter a subscription's resources by type, then get the resource group of the first match.
  name: Microsoft Azure Find Resources by Type and Inspect Their Group
  slug: microsoft-azure-find-resources-by-type-and-inspect-group-workflow
- description: Confirm a subscription is enabled, list its resource providers, then list its resources.
  name: Microsoft Azure List Providers and Resources
  slug: microsoft-azure-list-providers-and-resources-workflow
- description: List a subscription's resource groups, get the first one, and inventory its resources.
  name: Microsoft Azure List Resource Groups and Inventory Resources
  slug: microsoft-azure-list-resource-groups-and-inventory-resources-workflow
- description: Confirm a resource group exists, start a deployment into it, and poll until terminal.
  name: Microsoft Azure Redeploy Into an Existing Resource Group
  slug: microsoft-azure-redeploy-into-existing-group-workflow
- description: Read a subscription, create a region-tagged resource group, deploy into it, and poll to completion.
  name: Microsoft Azure Provision a Region-Specific Deployment
  slug: microsoft-azure-relocate-resource-group-deployment-workflow
- description: List resource groups, get the first, patch governance tags onto it, then inventory its resources.
  name: Microsoft Azure Resource Group Lifecycle Tag and Inventory
  slug: microsoft-azure-resource-group-lifecycle-tag-and-inventory-workflow
- description: List tenant subscriptions, get the first one's detail, then list its resource groups.
  name: Microsoft Azure Select Subscription and List Resource Groups
  slug: microsoft-azure-select-subscription-and-list-resource-groups-workflow
- description: Read a resource group, apply new tags with a patch, and confirm the tags were written.
  name: Microsoft Azure Tag an Existing Resource Group
  slug: microsoft-azure-tag-resource-group-workflow

finops:
- name: Microsoft Azure Finops
  service_category: Cloud Infrastructure
  slug: microsoft-azure-finops
image: https://azure.microsoft.com/svghandler/azure-logo.png
integrations:
- description: Native integration with GitHub Actions, GitHub Codespaces, and GitHub Advanced Security for DevOps workflows.
  name: GitHub
- description: Official Azure provider for Terraform enabling infrastructure as code for all Azure resources.
  name: Terraform
- description: Business analytics integration with embedded dashboards, Azure Synapse connectivity, and real-time streaming.
  name: Power BI
- description: Enterprise productivity integration with Azure AD, Microsoft Graph, and Office 365 services.
  name: Microsoft 365
- description: Certified SAP workload support with Azure Large Instances, SAP HANA, and integration services.
  name: SAP
- description: Managed Kubernetes with AKS, Azure Arc-enabled Kubernetes, and integration with CNCF ecosystem tools.
  name: Kubernetes
- description: Partnership for Azure Red Hat OpenShift and RHEL-based workloads on Azure infrastructure.
  name: Red Hat
- description: Azure VMware Solution for migrating VMware workloads to Azure without re-platforming.
  name: VMware

overview: 'Microsoft Azure publishes 1659 APIs on the [APIs.io](https://apis.io/) network, including AccessConnector API, AccessControlRecords API, AccessPolicies API, and 1656 more. Tagged areas include API Management, Cloud, Cloud Computing, Enterprise, and Infrastructure as a Service.


  The Microsoft Azure catalog on APIs.io includes 9 JSON-LD contexts and 2 Spectral governance rulesets.


  Microsoft Azure''s developer surface includes authentication, CLI, pricing, getting-started guide, engineering blog, support, developer portal, and 48 more developer resources.'

use_cases:
- description: Build and deploy microservices-based applications using AKS, Container Registry, and Azure API Management.
  name: Cloud-Native Applications
- description: Migrate and modernize databases with Azure SQL, Cosmos DB, and Azure Database Migration Service.
  name: Data Modernization
- description: Integrate GPT models, computer vision, and speech services into applications via Azure OpenAI and Cognitive Services.
  name: AI-Powered Applications
- description: Implement business continuity with Azure Site Recovery, geo-redundant storage, and cross-region replication.
  name: Disaster Recovery
- description: Connect, monitor, and manage IoT devices at scale with Azure IoT Hub, IoT Central, and Digital Twins.
  name: IoT Solutions
- description: Connect systems and applications with Azure Logic Apps, Service Bus, API Management, and Event Grid.
  name: Enterprise Integration
